HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001390/B004","root_001390/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1dd489deed97d1f66da2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:1\",\"root_001390\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yerinden ayrılmayan cesur kişi çağrışımı, son soruya önceki kararsızlık ve düşüş karşısında sarsılmaz bir duruş görüntüsü ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001390/B004","root_001390/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_200a7d616558d79462a6","text":"5. **The brave/stationary laysa branch creates a minor counter-image.** Lexical evidence: ل ي س B004, unflinching brave one; ل ي س B005, one who stays put; ق و م B002/B016, standing and stoppage. Structural cue: this is exploratory because أليس is grammatically the negating verb, but the packet has been saturated with standing, falling, stopping, and judgment. Assigned role: the root inventory lets the final negation carry a faint image of unflinching firmness: the question does not move from its position. Abductive move: I infer a resonance, not a primary sense, between grammatical negation and steadfast judicial stance. Reading change: the final question feels like a fixed point against the earlier wavering and descent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002","root_000504/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_322a6fb2ef6a2845ae6c","text":"2. **Judicial closure of the work-and-recompense sequence.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ك م/B002` supplies just adjudication among people, and the root occurs twice in superlative *aḥkam* and plural *ḥākimīn*. It is activated by 95:7’s `د ي ن/B002` reckoning and 95:6’s `ء ج ر/B001` recompense. **Structural cue:** judge language immediately answers the question about *dīn*. **Assigned role:** the first form ranks the deciding competence; the second supplies the comparison class of adjudicators. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies adjacency and repeated root; I infer that the named judge executes the account linking deed to return. **Reading change:** *aḥkami l-ḥākimīn* is active judicial closure, not merely a general title of wisdom.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002","root_000348/B005","root_000504/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3408b54e992e2aeab7eb","text":"4. **Delegated judgment is withdrawn into divine authority.** Lexical evidence: ح ك م B005, delegation/entrusting decision; د ي ن B007 in 95:7, crediting a person in judgment/oath or leaving to conscience; ء م ن B002, trusting assent. Structural cue: 95:7 confronts the addressee's denial; 95:8 asks whether Allah is not the final judge. Assigned role: human conscience and social adjudication are not abolished, but the last decision is entrusted to Allah. Abductive move: I infer that the question moves from human response to the authority competent to decide the dispute. Reading change: the ayah does not merely assert divine superiority; it reassigns the disputed case from denier to final court.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3ef7175aa3fec81e1ee1","text":"No later ayah remains in the packet. The retrospective effect instead comes from looking backward: 95:8 gathers the basmalah's divine name, 95:4's أحسن تقويم, 95:5's reversal, 95:6's wage/reward, and 95:7's الدين into a final adjudicating point. No additional correction emerged beyond preserving the coexistence of judgment-as-court, judgment-as-restraint, and judgment-as-wise/perfected measure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_519351abce75c31b017e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:2\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Başlangıçta anılan Allah adının sonda yeniden gelmesi, merhametle açılan çerçeveyi hükümle kapatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B001","root_000348/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_51acdcb8c56f13dffe49","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:3\",\"root_000348\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]],[\"95:8:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Allah'ın hükmü yalnız karar vermek değil, bozulmayı dizginleyip doğru olana geri çeviren etkin bir düzeltmedir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001390/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5380412156d7742b5f8e","text":"1. **An emphatic present affirmation formed through negation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ل ي س/B001` supplies present-state negation and explicitly notes the *bāʾ* in its predicate as intensifying that negation. **Structural cue:** interrogative *a-laysa* opens the clause, Allah is its raised subject, and *bi-aḥkami* realizes the emphatic predicate construction named by the branch. **Assigned role:** negation is the rhetorical frame whose denial is itself denied, forcing affirmation of the predicate now. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies grammar but not audience response; I infer that the question expects the preceding case to make “yes” unavoidable. **Reading change:** the ending is not an open request for information but a grammatically intensified affirmation of Allah’s unmatched judgment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000323/B001","root_000348/B003","root_000348/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6b6d9dcd265d87a58cf5","text":"3. **Judgment as wisdom/perfection matching best taqwim.** Lexical evidence: ح ك م B003, wisdom and rightly guided knowledge; ح ك م B004, making firm/perfected/decisive; ح س ن B001/B002 and ق و م B008 in 95:4, beauty/goodness and straightness. Structural cue: أحكم mirrors أحسن in form and evaluative force: \"best/most beautiful\" formation is answered by \"most wise/decisive\" judging. Assigned role: final حكم completes the initial تقويم by providing a perfected evaluative standard. Abductive move: I infer a formal echo between أحسن and أحكم, both activated by measure, beauty, and decisiveness. Reading change: 95:8 reads as the epistemic completion of 95:4: the one who proportioned best also judges most firmly.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7284118074540b66220c","text":"3. **Judgment as restraint that turns corruption back toward rightness.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ك م/B001` supplies restraining injustice or corruption and “turning back” to set right. Earlier 95:5 supplies `ر د د/B001` return and 95:6 supplies `ص ل ح/B001` repair against corruption. **Structural cue:** the same short sequence moves from return, through corrective deeds, to the final doubled judgment root. **Assigned role:** *ḥukm* is the bracing action that stops corrupt motion and directs it toward soundness. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies return, repair, and restraint but not the causal chain; I infer misdirection → restraint/return → setting right as one judicial mechanism. **Reading change:** “most *aḥkam*” can mean the most effective restrainer and corrector, not only the issuer of verdicts.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000348/B002","root_001390/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_72a24a39d5307f8b59c0","text":"1. **Negation as an emphatic closure of the whole argument.** Lexical evidence: ل ي س B001, fixed negating verb for present-state denial with bāʾ in the predicate for emphasis; ء ل ه B002, fixed divine name; ح ك م B002, judgment/adjudication. Structural cue: أليس frames the final question after 95:7's \"what makes you deny?\" Assigned role: laysa does not introduce uncertainty; it negates the possibility that Allah is other than the most decisive judge. Abductive move: I infer that the final interrogative turns denial back against itself by using a negation that expects recognition. Reading change: the ayah reads as judicial closure rather than a simple doctrinal question.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B001","root_000348/B006","root_000555/B002","root_000876/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7db7d3b384a9a7bb3ee6","text":"2. **Judgment as restraining corruption, not only passing sentence.** Lexical evidence: ح ك م B001, restraining and turning back to set right; ح ك م B006, bridle-piece that restrains; ر د د B002 in 95:5, repelling/preventing; ص ل ح B001 in 95:6, setting right. Structural cue: the root ح ك م appears doubled in superlative/plural form after fall, exception, and denial. Assigned role: الحكم is a bridle and restraint that prevents the downward/corruptive motion from becoming final chaos. Abductive move: I infer that the judge's role includes corrective restraint because the packet's fall and repair branches invite that function. Reading change: \"best of judges\" means the one whose judgment reins in corruption and redirects toward rightness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000348/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:6","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8be5cb6ea70e17fe8246","text":"6. **The question as an act of entrusting decision.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ك م/B005` supplies arbitration and entrusting a decision to another; fixed `ء ل ه/B001` supplies worship and the worshipped one, activated by 95:7’s `د ي ن/B001` obedience and worship. **Structural cue:** the rhetorical question asks the hearer to concede Allah’s status precisely at the point where the account requires a judge. **Assigned role:** the interrogative performs consent, *Allah* identifies the entrusted authority, and *ḥukm* names the accepted decision. **Abductive move:** the packet does not record an answer; I infer that assenting to the rhetorical question functions as submitting the dispute to the named adjudicator. **Reading change:** the final ayah does not only describe supreme judgment; it enacts the hearer’s arbitration and submission to it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000348/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a9c1076c984524abce97","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:2\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\"]],[\"95:8:3\",\"root_000348\",[\"B005\"]],[\"95:8:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Son soru, çekişmeli hesabı Allah'a teslim etme ve onun kararına boyun eğme eylemine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b7eff83f9b4dcff552bc","text":"5. **Truth-reaching knowledge against falsification.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ك م/B003` supplies knowledge and reason that hit the truth; 95:7’s `ك ذ ب/B001` supplies falsehood and `ك ذ ب/B002` declaring false. **Structural cue:** the truth-status question is followed immediately by the title of the one whose knowledge reaches the right result. **Assigned role:** wisdom is the epistemic capacity that detects false attribution and lands on truth before judgment is rendered. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies opposition but not an explicit investigation; I infer falsification claim → truth-discerning knowledge → sound verdict. **Reading change:** *aḥkam* includes epistemic reliability, so the judgment is presented as informed rather than merely powerful.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b9468a832644768b5017","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:3\",\"root_000348\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:8:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Son hüküm, önceki iş ile ödül arasındaki hesabı sonuçlandıran etkin yargı olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bc6a468709cf669bd7c1","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window, so no later surprise emerged. The retrospective pass retained the internal double use of `ح ك م` and the immediately preceding 95:7 only as already-recorded context, not as a later activation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000348/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cd5402d9bb646c804a5b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:2\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]],[\"95:8:3\",\"root_000348\",[\"B004\"]],[\"95:8:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İnsanın en iyi yapıda kurulması, sonunda onu kuran Allah'ın en sağlam ve kusursuz kararıyla karşılanır; yaratmadaki ölçü ile hükümdeki kesinlik tek yetkinlikte birleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B004","root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ded1890c338275973d7e","text":"4. **Perfected formation answered by perfected decision.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ح ك م/B004` supplies making a thing firm, perfected, decisive, and complete. Earlier 95:4 supplies measured creation (`خ ل ق/B001`) and straight proportion (`ق و م/B008`). **Structural cue:** “best setting” near the start of the focus window is answered by superlative *aḥkam* at its close, with descent and exception between them. **Assigned role:** creation establishes a perfected design; final *ḥukm* establishes a perfected and decisive resolution of what happens to that design. **Abductive move:** the packet does not identify one process as the other; I infer a compositional correspondence between precision in formation and precision in final determination. **Reading change:** the last title binds craftsmanship and judgment into one competence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000348/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ee68e451e4db200336e0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"95:8:3\",\"root_000348\",[\"B003\"]],[\"95:8:4\",\"root_000348\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hüküm, yalnız yetkeye değil, yalanlamanın içinden doğruyu bulup sonuca ulaştıran bilgi ve sağduyuya dayanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
